- **h-archive**: Archive STANDARD/RESEARCH task at Phase 6/R3 — gate → WAL → move → DONE. TRIGGER: '归档' / Phase 6 boundary. NOT FOR: PATCH (rejected); PR (use /h-pr). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-archive.md) · ★18)
- **h-brief**: Scaffold a new task_brief from schema + bidirectional bind to launch_spec ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-brief.md) · ★18)
- **h-ci**: Ingest CI/CD failure → classify → route (debug task / incident) + failure_memory record. TRIGGER: red GitHub Actions run / 'CI 挂了'. NOT FOR: local test fail (use /h-fix-bug). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-ci.md) · ★18)
- **h-collab**: Generate a cross-team collaboration deliverable from task_brief — draft document, create collab state file, mark task pending external review ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-collab.md) · ★18)
- **h-collab-update**: Log external team feedback on a collab deliverable — record questions/answers, update open items, optionally sign off to unblock Implement ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-collab-update.md) · ★18)
- **h-context-check**: Pull-model context probe — recent events + recurring failures + active task + dirty diff + sensitive-surface scan + active insights. The canonical entry to gather context before deciding profile / scope / next action. TRIGGER: phase start / 不确定状态 / 进入未知领域. NOT FOR: every prompt (this is on-demand, not push). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-context-check.md) · ★18)
- **h-continue-batch**: Continue a batch of GitHub pull requests, accounting for their relationships. ([Novirmor/homonto](https://github.com/Novirmor/homonto/blob/HEAD/catalog/commands/h-continue-batch.md) · ★3)
- **h-continue-pr**: Continue a GitHub PR — address feedback, verify fixes, and update the same PR without requiring an engine. ([Novirmor/homonto](https://github.com/Novirmor/homonto/blob/HEAD/catalog/commands/h-continue-pr.md) · ★3)

([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-decompose.md) · ★18)
- **h-design**: Design step for HIGH-risk brief — dispatch system-architect, write ADRs, fill §8/§9. TRIGGER: HIGH-risk after /h-brief / '做设计'. NOT FOR: implementation. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-design.md) · ★18)
- **h-distill**: Wiki cleanup — librarian scan → user approves → execute. TRIGGER: explicit user request ('整理 wiki' / 'clean stale wiki') or after `distill_threshold.py` reports growth thresholds tripped. NOT FOR: doc cleanup (use documentation-curator). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-distill.md) · ★18)
- **h-distill-from-code**: Reconcile wiki claims against current code, bounded scope. TRIGGER: 'wiki 过时了 校对一下' / 'reconcile'. NOT FOR: WAL at Archive (use /h-archive). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-distill-from-code.md) · ★18)
- **h-evolve**: Turn a high-confidence insight into a concrete rule-change proposal. Pick insight → generate file/line edit suggestion → human approves → optional --apply. TRIGGER: agent or user wants to act on an active insight. NOT FOR: routine insight viewing (use /h-context-check). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-evolve.md) · ★18)
- **h-fix-bug**: Bug → root-cause-first pipeline; production bugs get incident record. No code until root cause locked. TRIGGER: bug report / 'P1 down'. NOT FOR: CI fail (use /h-ci). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-fix-bug.md) · ★18)
- **h-from-ticket**: Ingest GitHub Issue / Jira / Linear ticket → task_brief + ambiguity check + launch_spec row. TRIGGER: ticket URL pasted. NOT FOR: PRD/EPIC (use /h-decompose). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-from-ticket.md) · ★18)
- **h-gates**: Full gate suite audit across accumulated diff — phase-aware, severity-aggregated. TRIGGER: phase transition / '过门'. NOT FOR: per-edit (hook handles). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-gates.md) · ★18)
- **h-help**: Scenario-driven menu of /h-* commands. Use when you're not sure which command fits your current situation. TRIGGER: user asks "which command should I use" / "help" / "怎么用" / new user onboarding. NOT FOR: detailed how-to of a specific command (read that command's own file). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-help.md) · ★18)
- **h-incident**: Capture production incident → wiki/incidents/&lt;date&gt;_&lt;slug&gt;.md with future-LLM reminder line. TRIGGER: Sentry alert / '记录事故'. NOT FOR: bug pipeline (use /h-fix-bug). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-incident.md) · ★18)
- **h-open-node**: Find a Hadron node by name and open it in the portal (hadronmemory.com) ([hadron-memory/hadron-plugins](https://github.com/hadron-memory/hadron-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/hadron/commands/h-open-node.md))
- **h-pr**: Create a pull request from the current task — pre-gate, build PR body from task_brief, gh pr create, write PR number back to launch_spec ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-pr.md) · ★18)
- **h-publish-insight**: Manually convert a local insight into a git-tracked team knowledge doc under .claude/wiki/insights/. Bridges single-machine expert system → team-shared learning. TRIGGER: user explicit invoke ONLY (never auto-fired). NOT FOR: rule changes (use /h-evolve), routine viewing (use /h-context-check). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-publish-insight.md) · ★18)
- **h-reflect**: Session reflection — multi-select lessons (incident/wiki/failure/success/memory) → reset counters. TRIGGER: explicit '复盘' request, or `reflect_threshold.py` reports session_stats thresholds tripped. NOT FOR: archive (use /h-archive). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-reflect.md) · ★18)
- **h-release**: Release pipeline — version bump + WAL changelog + tag/push + CI check. TRIGGER: '发版' / Scenario RELEASE. NOT FOR: single PR merge (use /h-pr). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-release.md) · ★18)
- **h-research**: Scaffold research_report.md (no code) — Phase R1 entry. TRIGGER: prompt contains research verb (analyze / feasibility / evaluate / 调研) / @research shortcut. NOT FOR: code change (use /h-brief). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-research.md) · ★18)

- **habits**: Habit tracking. First run interviews you one question at a time to build a versioned habits profile — each habit with its own criteria (daily / N-per-week / N-per-month, build or limit) and a priority. Day-to-day tracking lives in dated markdown files (life/habit-tracking/&lt;date&gt;.md) you tick like a checklist; a SQLite store is synced from them for analysis. Afterward it shows your progress vs each criteria (✅ met / ⏳ not yet / ⚠️ over), top 20 by priority. Habits are auto-marked from your journaling + planning sessions. - **handoff**: The one hand-off command. Asks who is receiving the work, then delivers it — (1) everything including a vendored Product Builder, so they keep building; (3) Figma, lowered deterministically from the registry and written through the Figma MCP, with the DS Bridge plugin as the fallback; (4) tickets for front-end developers, one file per buildable slice in a fixed template, linted, then published one-way to Notion or Jira. Mode 2 (engineering) was retired in v3.0.0 and the numbers were not reused. - **handoff**: Print one block the owner pastes into a new terminal to open a fresh session continuing this one: one command that changes into the checkout and runs the target agent harness's start command with the continuation prompt inline (objective, anchored Issue, revision, state and next act re-derived from the tracker). The target defaults to the current agent harness. With no argument, when this session's last message asks the owner to do something in another agent harness, the block targets that harness with the ask inline instead, and this session keeps running.
